L0 extraction: quick metadata extraction from session (files, brief summary)\nimport type { ChatMessage, L0Data } from './types.js';\n\n// Regex для поиска путей к файлам в тексте (e.g. src/foo.mjs, ./bar/baz.ts, package.json)\nconst FILE_PATH_RE = /(?:^|[\\s`\"'(])([.\\w/-]+\\.\\w{1,10})(?=[\\s`\"'),;:]|$)/gm;\nconst MAX_SUMMARY_LEN = 120;\n\n// Паттерны для извлечения tool invocations / команд из текста\nconst TOOL_USE_RE = /```(?:bash|sh|shell)\\s*([\\s\\S]*?)```/gm;\nconst BASH_CMD_RE = /\\$\\s+([\\w./][\\w./-]*(?:\\s+[\\w./-]+)*)/gm;\n\n// Паттерны для извлечения строк ошибок\nconst ERROR_RE = /(?:Error|error|ERROR|exception|Exception|FATAL|fatal|failed|Failed|FAILED):\\s*(.{10,120})/gm;\n\n// Паттерны для извлечения неудачных подходов\nconst FAILURE_PATTERNS = [\n   /(?:не сработал[оа]?|didn'?t work|not work|does not work)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n   /(?:откатил|reverted?|rolled? back)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n   /(?:не помогло|не решило|didn'?t help|didn'?t fix)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n   /(?:пробовал[аи]?\\s+[^,.]+,?\\s*но)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n   /(?:tried\\s+[^,.]+,?\\s*but)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n   /(?:approach\\s+failed|подход\\s+не\\s+сработал)[^.]*[.!]/gim,\n];\n\nexport function extractFilePaths(text: string): string[] {\n   const matches = new Set<string>();\n   for (const m of text.matchAll(FILE_PATH_RE)) {\n      const path = m[1];\n      if (path && (path.includes('/') || path.includes('.'))) matches.add(path);\n   }\n   return [...matches].filter((p) => !p.startsWith('http') && !p.startsWith('//'));\n}\n\n/** Извлечь команды из bash-блоков и $ ... строк */\nexport function extractCommands(text: string): string[] {\n   const cmds = new Set<string>();\n   for (const m of text.matchAll(TOOL_USE_RE)) {\n      const block = m[1]?.trim();\n      if (block) {\n         // Берём первую строку каждого блока как команду\n         const firstLine = block.split('\\n')[0]?.trim();\n         if (firstLine) cmds.add(firstLine.slice(0, 80));\n      }\n   }\n   for (const m of text.matchAll(BASH_CMD_RE)) {\n      const cmd = m[1]?.trim();\n      if (cmd) cmds.add(cmd.slice(0, 80));\n   }\n   return [...cmds].slice(0, 20);\n}\n\n/** Извлечь строки ошибок из текста */\nexport function extractErrors(text: string): string[] {\n   const errors = new Set<string>();\n   for (const m of text.matchAll(ERROR_RE)) {\n      const msg = m[1]?.trim();\n      if (msg) errors.add(msg.slice(0, 120));\n   }\n   return [...errors].slice(0, 10);\n}\n\n/** Извлечь неудачные подходы из текста */\nexport function extractFailures(text: string): string[] {\n   const failures = new Set<string>();\n   for (const pattern of FAILURE_PATTERNS) {\n      // Сбрасываем lastIndex для каждого regex\n      pattern.lastIndex = 0;\n      for (const m of text.matchAll(pattern)) {\n         const msg = m[0]?.trim();\n         if (msg && msg.length > 15) failures.add(msg.slice(0, 200));\n      }\n   }\n   return [...failures].slice(0, 10);\n}\n\n/** Извлечь следующий шаг из последнего сообщения ассистента */\nexport function extractNextStep(messages: ChatMessage[]): string | undefined {\n   // Берём последнее сообщение ассистента\n   const lastAssistant = [...messages].reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'assistant');\n   if (!lastAssistant) return undefined;\n   const text = lastAssistant.content;\n\n   // Ищем явные маркеры следующего шага\n   const nextStepPatterns = [/(?:следующий шаг|next step|далее нужно|теперь нужно|осталось)[:\\s]+(.{20,200})/i, /(?:TODO|FIXME|нужно доделать|остаётся)[:\\s]+(.{20,200})/i];\n   for (const pattern of nextStepPatterns) {\n      const match = text.match(pattern);\n      if (match?.[1]) return match[1].trim().slice(0, 200);\n   }\n\n   // Если явного маркера нет — берём первое предложение последнего ответа как резюме\n   const firstSentence = text.match(/^[^.!?\\n]{20,200}[.!?]/);\n   if (firstSentence) return firstSentence[0].trim();\n\n   return undefined;\n}\n\nexport function extractL0FromMessages(messages: ChatMessage[], project: string, agentId?: string): L0Data {\n   if (!messages.length) return { summary: '', project, messageCount: 0, files: [], topics: [], agent: agentId };\n\n   const firstUser = messages.find((m) => m.role === 'user');\n   const summary = firstUser ? firstUser.content.replace(/\\n/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, MAX_SUMMARY_LEN) : '';\n\n   const files = new Set<string>();\n   const commands = new Set<string>();\n   const errors = new Set<string>();\n   const failures = new Set<string>();\n\n   // Для вычисления duration — собираем timestamps (если сообщения имеют ts)\n   // Базовый вариант: используем Date.now() как timestamp (перезаписывается парсерами агентов)\n   for (const msg of messages) {\n      const text = typeof msg.content === 'string' ? msg.content : '';\n      for (const f of extractFilePaths(text)) files.add(f);\n      for (const c of extractCommands(text)) commands.add(c);\n      for (const e of extractErrors(text)) errors.add(e);\n      for (const fl of extractFailures(text)) failures.add(fl);\n   }\n\n   // Извлечь следующий шаг из последнего ответа ассистента\n   const next_step = extractNextStep(messages);\n\n   return {\n      summary,\n      project,\n      messageCount: messages.length,\n      files: [...files].slice(0, 20),\n      timestamp: Date.now(),\n      agent: agentId,\n      commands: commands.size > 0 ? [...commands] : undefined,\n      errors: errors.size > 0 ? [...errors] : undefined,\n      failures: failures.size > 0 ? [...failures] : undefined,\n      next_step,\n   };\n}\n\n/** Content block from JSONL (text block) */\ninterface ContentBlock {\n   type: string;\n   text?: string;\n}\n\n/** Event from a session JSONL file */\ninterface JSONLEvent {\n   type: string;\n   message?: {\n      content: string | ContentBlock[];\n   };\n}\n\nexport function extractL0FromJSONL(lines: string[], project: string): L0Data {\n   const messages: ChatMessage[] = [];\n   for (const line of lines) {\n      try {\n         const event = JSON.parse(line) as JSONLEvent;\n         if (event.type === 'human' && event.message?.content) {\n            const text =\n               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n                  ? event.message.content\n                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n                       .join(' ');\n            messages.push({ role: 'user', content: text });\n         }\n         if (event.type === 'assistant' && event.message?.content) {\n            const text =\n               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n                  ? event.message.content\n                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n                       .join(' ');\n            messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: text });\n         }\n      } catch {\n         // Skip malformed lines\n      }\n   }\n   return extractL0FromMessages(messages, project);\n}\n","numLines":180,"startLine":1,"totalLines":180}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b04f719c-0d0c-425d-9ddb-6a678270f124","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"59035361-f1b6-417a-8f79-c0f8b2dad02a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0156SsAgZcMJFzT64TksYNjh","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// L1 extraction: deep memory extraction via LLM (multi-agent support)\n2\timport { execFileSync, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';\n3\timport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\n4\timport { join } from 'node:path';\n5\timport { resolveCandidate } from './dedup.js';\n6\timport { serializeMemory } from './format.js';\n7\timport { readMemoryConfig } from './config.js';\n8\timport type { ChatMessage, MemoryCandidate, MemoryCategory, MemoryEntry } from './types.js';\n9\timport { readIndex, writeIndex, acquireLock, releaseLock } from './index.js';\n10\t\n11\t/** Supported agent identifiers for session file discovery */\n12\ttype AgentId = 'claude' | 'codex' | 'qwen' | 'companion' | 'gemini';\n13\t\n14\tconst VALID_CATEGORIES: MemoryCategory[] = ['profile', 'preferences', 'entities', 'events', 'cases', 'patterns'];\n15\t\n16\t// Sanitize LLM-generated names for safe use in paths and keys\n17\tfunction sanitizeName(name: string): string {\n18\t   return (\n19\t      name\n20\t         .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/gi, '-')\n21\t         .replace(/-+/g, '-')\n22\t         .replace(/^-|-$/g, '')\n23\t         .slice(0, 50) || 'unnamed'\n24\t   );\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\tconst EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT = 60_000;\n28\t// HEAD_COUNT + TAIL_COUNT = 50 сообщений — известное ограничение для длинных сессий:\n29\t// средина разговора не попадает в extraction. Достаточно для большинства сессий.\n30\tconst HEAD_COUNT = 15;\n31\tconst TAIL_COUNT = 35;\n32\t\n33\texport function buildExtractionPrompt(messages: ChatMessage[]): string {\n34\t   const conversation = messages.map((m) => `${m.role}: ${m.content}`).join('\\n\\n');\n35\t   return `Extract structured memories from this Claude Code session conversation.\n36\t\n37\tReturn a JSON array of memories. Each memory:\n38\t{\n39\t  \"category\": one of: profile, preferences, entities, events, cases, patterns\n40\t  \"name\": kebab-case identifier (e.g. \"auth-token-fix\")\n41\t  \"content\": 1-3 sentences of useful information to remember\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\tCategories:\n45\t- profile: user role, expertise, responsibilities\n46\t- preferences: coding style, tools, workflow\n47\t- entities: projects, services, people, systems\n48\t- events: incidents, deployments, decisions (with dates and reasoning)\n49\t- cases: problem + solution pairs (IMPORTANT: also extract FAILED approaches — what was tried and why it didn't work, so future sessions don't repeat the same mistakes)\n50\t- patterns: recurring approaches, anti-patterns\n51\t\n52\tPay special attention to:\n53\t1. FAILED APPROACHES — if something was tried and didn't work, extract it as a \"cases\" memory with clear explanation of WHY it failed. This prevents wasting time retrying.\n54\t2. DECISIONS — architectural or technical decisions with reasoning (why X was chosen over Y).\n55\t3. NEXT STEPS — if work is incomplete, what should be done next.\n56\t\n57\tOnly extract information worth remembering in future sessions. Skip trivial exchanges.\n58\tIf nothing is worth remembering, return an empty array [].\n59\t\n60\tConversation:\n61\t${conversation}\n62\t\n63\tJSON array:`;\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\texport function parseLLMResponse(response: string): MemoryCandidate[] {\n67\t   // Пустой ответ → пустой массив\n68\t   if (!response || !response.trim()) return [];\n69\t   try {\n70\t      // Ищем JSON-массив объектов: первый '[{' паттерн, избегая markdown [link](url)\n71\t      const match = response.match(/\\[\\s*\\{[\\s\\S]*\\]/);\n72\t      if (!match) return [];\n73\t      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(match[0]);\n74\t      if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];\n75\t      const result: MemoryCandidate[] = [];\n76\t      for (const m of parsed as unknown[]) {\n77\t         const obj = m as Record<string, unknown>;\n78\t         // Валидация схемы: category и name — непустые строки, content — непустая строка\n79\t         if (\n80\t            typeof obj.category !== 'string' ||\n81\t            !obj.category.trim() ||\n82\t            typeof obj.name !== 'string' ||\n83\t            !obj.name.trim() ||\n84\t            typeof obj.content !== 'string' ||\n85\t            !obj.content.trim()\n86\t         ) {\n87\t            // Невалидный кандидат — пропустить\n88\t            continue;\n89\t         }\n90\t         if (!VALID_CATEGORIES.includes(obj.category as MemoryCategory)) {\n91\t            // Неизвестная категория — пропустить\n92\t            continue;\n93\t         }\n94\t         result.push(obj as unknown as MemoryCandidate);\n95\t      }\n96\t      return result;\n97\t   } catch {\n98\t      return [];\n99\t   }\n100\t}\n101\t\n102\t/** Content block from JSONL */\n103\tinterface ContentBlock {\n104\t   type: string;\n105\t   text?: string;\n106\t}\n107\t\n108\t/** Event from a JSONL file */\n109\tinterface JSONLEvent {\n110\t   type: string;\n111\t   message?: {\n112\t      content: string | ContentBlock[];\n113\t   };\n114\t}\n115\t\n116\tfunction extractMessagesFromJSONL(jsonlPath: string): ChatMessage[] {\n117\t   const content = readFileSync(jsonlPath, 'utf8');\n118\t   const lines = content.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n119\t   const messages: ChatMessage[] = [];\n120\t\n121\t   for (const line of lines) {\n122\t      try {\n123\t         const event = JSON.parse(line) as JSONLEvent;\n124\t         if (event.type === 'human' && event.message?.content) {\n125\t            const text =\n126\t               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n127\t                  ? event.message.content\n128\t                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n129\t                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n130\t                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n131\t                       .join(' ');\n132\t            messages.push({ role: 'user', content: text.slice(0, 1000) });\n133\t         }\n134\t         if (event.type === 'assistant' && event.message?.content) {\n135\t            const text =\n136\t               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n137\t                  ? event.message.content\n138\t                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n139\t                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n140\t                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n141\t                       .join(' ');\n142\t            messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: text.slice(0, 1500) });\n143\t         }\n144\t      } catch {\n145\t         // Skip malformed lines\n146\t      }\n147\t   }\n148\t\n149\t   // Extract head + tail\n150\t   if (messages.length <= HEAD_COUNT + TAIL_COUNT) return messages;\n151\t   return [...messages.slice(0, HEAD_COUNT), ...messages.slice(-TAIL_COUNT)];\n152\t}\n153\t\n154\t// Search for session JSONL in a flat directory of project subdirs (claude format)\n155\tfunction findClaudeSessionJSONL(projectsDir: string, sessionId: string): string | null {\n156\t   try {\n157\t      const dirs = readdirSync(projectsDir);\n158\t      for (const dir of dirs) {\n159\t         const p = join(projectsDir, dir, sessionId + '.jsonl');\n160\t         if (existsSync(p)) return p;\n161\t      }\n162\t   } catch {\n163\t      // Directory may not exist\n164\t   }\n165\t   return null;\n166\t}\n167\t\n168\t// Recursively search for files containing sessionId in their name\n169\tfunction findFileRecursive(dir: string, sessionId: string): string | null {\n170\t   if (!existsSync(dir)) return null;\n171\t   try {\n172\t      const entries = readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n173\t      for (const entry of entries) {\n174\t         const fullPath = join(dir, entry.name);\n175\t         if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n176\t            const found = findFileRecursive(fullPath, sessionId);\n177\t            if (found) return found;\n178\t         } else if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.includes(sessionId) && entry.name.endsWith('.jsonl')) {\n179\t            return fullPath;\n180\t         }\n181\t      }\n182\t   } catch {\n183\t      // Permission or read errors\n184\t   }\n185\t   return null;\n186\t}\n187\t\n188\t// Find session JSONL in qwen format: ~/.qwen/projects/{dir}/chats/{sessionId}.jsonl\n189\tfunction findQwenSessionJSONL(sessionId: string): string | null {\n190\t   const qwenProjects = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.qwen', 'projects');\n191\t   if (!existsSync(qwenProjects)) return null;\n192\t   try {\n193\t      const dirs = readdirSync(qwenProjects);\n194\t      for (const dir of dirs) {\n195\t         const p = join(qwenProjects, dir, 'chats', sessionId + '.jsonl');\n196\t         if (existsSync(p)) return p;\n197\t      }\n198\t   } catch {\n199\t      // Directory may not exist\n200\t   }\n201\t   return null;\n202\t}\n203\t\n204\t// Find session JSONL for companion: ~/.companion/recordings/{sessionId}*.jsonl\n205\tfunction findCompanionSessionJSONL(sessionId: string): string | null {\n206\t   const recordingsDir = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.companion', 'recordings');\n207\t   if (!existsSync(recordingsDir)) return null;\n208\t   try {\n209\t      const files = readdirSync(recordingsDir);\n210\t      const match = files.find((f) => f.startsWith(sessionId) && f.endsWith('.jsonl'));\n211\t      if (match) return join(recordingsDir, match);\n212\t   } catch {\n213\t      // Directory may not exist\n214\t   }\n215\t   return null;\n216\t}\n217\t\n218\t/**\n219\t * Find session JSONL file based on agent type.\n220\t * Each agent stores sessions in a different location and format.\n221\t */\n222\tfunction findSessionJSONL(projectsDir: string, sessionId: string, agentId: AgentId = 'claude'): string | null {\n223\t   switch (agentId) {\n224\t      case 'claude':\n225\t         return findClaudeSessionJSONL(projectsDir, sessionId);\n226\t      case 'codex': {\n227\t         const codexSessions = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.codex', 'sessions');\n228\t         return findFileRecursive(codexSessions, sessionId);\n229\t      }\n230\t      case 'qwen':\n231\t         return findQwenSessionJSONL(sessionId);\n232\t      case 'companion':\n233\t         return findCompanionSessionJSONL(sessionId);\n234\t      case 'gemini':\n235\t         // Gemini has no JSONL session files\n236\t         return null;\n237\t      default:\n238\t         return null;\n239\t L1 extraction: deep memory extraction via LLM (multi-agent support)\nimport { execFileSync, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { join } from 'node:path';\nimport { resolveCandidate } from './dedup.js';\nimport { serializeMemory } from './format.js';\nimport { readMemoryConfig } from './config.js';\nimport type { ChatMessage, MemoryCandidate, MemoryCategory, MemoryEntry } from './types.js';\nimport { readIndex, writeIndex, acquireLock, releaseLock } from './index.js';\n\n/** Supported agent identifiers for session file discovery */\ntype AgentId = 'claude' | 'codex' | 'qwen' | 'companion' | 'gemini';\n\nconst VALID_CATEGORIES: MemoryCategory[] = ['profile', 'preferences', 'entities', 'events', 'cases', 'patterns'];\n\n// Sanitize LLM-generated names for safe use in paths and keys\nfunction sanitizeName(name: string): string {\n   return (\n      name\n         .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/gi, '-')\n         .replace(/-+/g, '-')\n         .replace(/^-|-$/g, '')\n         .slice(0, 50) || 'unnamed'\n   );\n}\n\nconst EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT = 60_000;\n// HEAD_COUNT + TAIL_COUNT = 50 сообщений — известное ограничение для длинных сессий:\n// средина разговора не попадает в extraction. Достаточно для большинства сессий.\nconst HEAD_COUNT = 15;\nconst TAIL_COUNT = 35;\n\nexport function buildExtractionPrompt(messages: ChatMessage[]): string {\n   const conversation = messages.map((m) => `${m.role}: ${m.content}`).join('\\n\\n');\n   return `Extract structured memories from this Claude Code session conversation.\n\nReturn a JSON array of memories. Each memory:\n{\n  \"category\": one of: profile, preferences, entities, events, cases, patterns\n  \"name\": kebab-case identifier (e.g. \"auth-token-fix\")\n  \"content\": 1-3 sentences of useful information to remember\n}\n\nCategories:\n- profile: user role, expertise, responsibilities\n- preferences: coding style, tools, workflow\n- entities: projects, services, people, systems\n- events: incidents, deployments, decisions (with dates and reasoning)\n- cases: problem + solution pairs (IMPORTANT: also extract FAILED approaches — what was tried and why it didn't work, so future sessions don't repeat the same mistakes)\n- patterns: recurring approaches, anti-patterns\n\nPay special attention to:\n1. FAILED APPROACHES — if something was tried and didn't work, extract it as a \"cases\" memory with clear explanation of WHY it failed. This prevents wasting time retrying.\n2. DECISIONS — architectural or technical decisions with reasoning (why X was chosen over Y).\n3. NEXT STEPS — if work is incomplete, what should be done next.\n\nOnly extract information worth remembering in future sessions. Skip trivial exchanges.\nIf nothing is worth remembering, return an empty array [].\n\nConversation:\n${conversation}\n\nJSON array:`;\n}\n\nexport function parseLLMResponse(response: string): MemoryCandidate[] {\n   // Пустой ответ → пустой массив\n   if (!response || !response.trim()) return [];\n   try {\n      // Ищем JSON-массив объектов: первый '[{' паттерн, избегая markdown [link](url)\n      const match = response.match(/\\[\\s*\\{[\\s\\S]*\\]/);\n      if (!match) return [];\n      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(match[0]);\n      if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];\n      const result: MemoryCandidate[] = [];\n      for (const m of parsed as unknown[]) {\n         const obj = m as Record<string, unknown>;\n         // Валидация схемы: category и name — непустые строки, content — непустая строка\n         if (\n            typeof obj.category !== 'string' ||\n            !obj.category.trim() ||\n            typeof obj.name !== 'string' ||\n            !obj.name.trim() ||\n            typeof obj.content !== 'string' ||\n            !obj.content.trim()\n         ) {\n            // Невалидный кандидат — пропустить\n            continue;\n         }\n         if (!VALID_CATEGORIES.includes(obj.category as MemoryCategory)) {\n            // Неизвестная категория — пропустить\n            continue;\n         }\n         result.push(obj as unknown as MemoryCandidate);\n      }\n      return result;\n   } catch {\n      return [];\n   }\n}\n\n/** Content block from JSONL */\ninterface ContentBlock {\n   type: string;\n   text?: string;\n}\n\n/** Event from a JSONL file */\ninterface JSONLEvent {\n   type: string;\n   message?: {\n      content: string | ContentBlock[];\n   };\n}\n\nfunction extractMessagesFromJSONL(jsonlPath: string): ChatMessage[] {\n   const content = readFileSync(jsonlPath, 'utf8');\n   const lines = content.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n   const messages: ChatMessage[] = [];\n\n   for (const line of lines) {\n      try {\n         const event = JSON.parse(line) as JSONLEvent;\n         if (event.type === 'human' && event.message?.content) {\n            const text =\n               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n                  ? event.message.content\n                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n                       .join(' ');\n            messages.push({ role: 'user', content: text.slice(0, 1000) });\n         }\n         if (event.type === 'assistant' && event.message?.content) {\n            const text =\n               typeof event.message.content === 'string'\n                  ? event.message.content\n                  : (event.message.content as ContentBlock[])\n                       .filter((b) => b.type === 'text')\n                       .map((b) => b.text ?? '')\n                       .join(' ');\n            messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: text.slice(0, 1500) });\n         }\n      } catch {\n         // Skip malformed lines\n      }\n   }\n\n   // Extract head + tail\n   if (messages.length <= HEAD_COUNT + TAIL_COUNT) return messages;\n   return [...messages.slice(0, HEAD_COUNT), ...messages.slice(-TAIL_COUNT)];\n}\n\n// Search for session JSONL in a flat directory of project subdirs (claude format)\nfunction findClaudeSessionJSONL(projectsDir: string, sessionId: string): string | null {\n   try {\n      const dirs = readdirSync(projectsDir);\n      for (const dir of dirs) {\n         const p = join(projectsDir, dir, sessionId + '.jsonl');\n         if (existsSync(p)) return p;\n      }\n   } catch {\n      // Directory may not exist\n   }\n   return null;\n}\n\n// Recursively search for files containing sessionId in their name\nfunction findFileRecursive(dir: string, sessionId: string): string | null {\n   if (!existsSync(dir)) return null;\n   try {\n      const entries = readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n      for (const entry of entries) {\n         const fullPath = join(dir, entry.name);\n         if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n            const found = findFileRecursive(fullPath, sessionId);\n            if (found) return found;\n         } else if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.includes(sessionId) && entry.name.endsWith('.jsonl')) {\n            return fullPath;\n         }\n      }\n   } catch {\n      // Permission or read errors\n   }\n   return null;\n}\n\n// Find session JSONL in qwen format: ~/.qwen/projects/{dir}/chats/{sessionId}.jsonl\nfunction findQwenSessionJSONL(sessionId: string): string | null {\n   const qwenProjects = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.qwen', 'projects');\n   if (!existsSync(qwenProjects)) return null;\n   try {\n      const dirs = readdirSync(qwenProjects);\n      for (const dir of dirs) {\n         const p = join(qwenProjects, dir, 'chats', sessionId + '.jsonl');\n         if (existsSync(p)) return p;\n      }\n   } catch {\n      // Directory may not exist\n   }\n   return null;\n}\n\n// Find session JSONL for companion: ~/.companion/recordings/{sessionId}*.jsonl\nfunction findCompanionSessionJSONL(sessionId: string): string | null {\n   const recordingsDir = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.companion', 'recordings');\n   if (!existsSync(recordingsDir)) return null;\n   try {\n      const files = readdirSync(recordingsDir);\n      const match = files.find((f) => f.startsWith(sessionId) && f.endsWith('.jsonl'));\n      if (match) return join(recordingsDir, match);\n   } catch {\n      // Directory may not exist\n   }\n   return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Find session JSONL file based on agent type.\n * Each agent stores sessions in a different location and format.\n */\nfunction findSessionJSONL(projectsDir: string, sessionId: string, agentId: AgentId = 'claude'): string | null {\n   switch (agentId) {\n      case 'claude':\n         return findClaudeSessionJSONL(projectsDir, sessionId);\n      case 'codex': {\n         const codexSessions = join(process.env.HOME || '', '.codex', 'sessions');\n         return findFileRecursive(codexSessions, sessionId);\n      }\n      case 'qwen':\n         return findQwenSessionJSONL(sessionId);\n      case 'companion':\n         return findCompanionSessionJSONL(sessionId);\n      case 'gemini':\n         // Gemini has no JSONL session files\n         return null;\n      default:\n         return null;\n   }\n}\n\n/** Синхронная пауза в миллисекундах */\nfunction sleepSync(ms: number): void {\n   Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);\n}\n\nfunction findClaudeCli(): string | null {\n   // Check standard locations first\n   const candidates = ['/usr/local/bin/claude', '/opt/homebrew/bin/claude'];\n   for (const c of candidates) {\n      if (existsSync(c)) return c;\n   }\n   // Fall back to which\n   try {\n      return execFileSync('which', ['claude'], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();\n   } catch {\n      // CLI not found\n   }\n   return null;\n}\n\n// Main extraction function (runs as a separate process)\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n   const [, , sessionId, project, rawAgentId] = process.argv;\n   if (!sessionId) process.exit(0);\n   const agentId: AgentId = (['claude', 'codex', 'qwen', 'companion', 'gemini'] as const).includes(rawAgentId as AgentId) ? (rawAgentId as AgentId) : 'claude';\n\n   // Gemini has no JSONL — записать l1_skipped флаг и завершить extraction\n   if (agentId === 'gemini') {\n      const memDirG = process.env.MEMORY_DIR || join(process.env.HOME || '', '.claude', 'session-memory');\n      const indexPathG = join(memDirG, 'index.json');\n      const lockPathG = join(memDirG, 'index.lock');\n      if (acquireLock(lockPathG)) {\n         try {\n            const idx = readIndex(indexPathG);\n            if (idx.sessions?.[sessionId]) {\n               idx.sessions[sessionId].l1_skipped = true;\n               writeIndex(indexPathG, idx);\n            }\n         } catch {\n            // Игнорируем ошибки при записи флага\n         } finally {\n            releaseLock(lockPathG);\n         }\n      }\n      return;\n   }\n\n   const memoryDir = process.env.MEMORY_DIR || join(process.env.HOME || '', '.claude', 'session-memory');\n   const indexPath = join(memoryDir, 'index.json');\n   const lockPath = join(memoryDir, 'index.lock');\n   const memoriesDir = join(memoryDir, 'memories');\n   const configPath = join(memoryDir, 'config.json');\n   const errorLog = join(memoryDir, 'error.log');\n   const projectsDir = process.env.PROJECTS_DIR || join(process.env.HOME || '', '.claude', 'projects');\n\n   if (!acquireLock(lockPath)) process.exit(0);\n\n   try {\n      const index = readIndex(indexPath);\n      const session = index.sessions?.[sessionId];\n      if (!session || session.l1_ready) {\n         releaseLock(lockPath);\n         return;\n      }\n\n      const jsonlPath = findSessionJSONL(projectsDir, sessionId, agentId);\n      if (!jsonlPath) {\n         releaseLock(lockPath);\n         return;\n      }\n\n      const messages = extractMessagesFromJSONL(jsonlPath);\n      if (messages.length === 0) {\n         releaseLock(lockPath);\n         return;\n      }\n\n      const memConfig = readMemoryConfig(configPath);\n      const model = memConfig.extractionModel || 'haiku';\n\n      const claudeCli = findClaudeCli();\n      if (!claudeCli) throw new Error('Claude CLI not found');\n\n      const prompt = buildExtractionPrompt(messages);\n      const spawnArgs = ['--model', model, '--print', '--output-format', 'text'] as const;\n      const spawnOpts = { input: prompt, timeout: EXTRACTION_TIMEOUT, encoding: 'utf8' as const, maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 };\n\n      let proc = spawnSync(claudeCli, [...spawnArgs], spawnOpts);\n      // Retry один раз при ошибке spawnSync (сбой процесса, timeout и т.п.)\n      if (proc.error || proc.status !== 0) {\n         sleepSync(2000);\n         proc = spawnSync(claudeCli, [...spawnArgs], spawnOpts);\n      }\n      if (proc.error) throw proc.error;\n      if (proc.status !== 0) throw new Error(proc.stderr || 'Claude CLI failed');\n      const result = proc.stdout;\n\n      const candidates = parseLLMResponse(result);\n\n      for (const candidate of candidates) {\n         candidate.name = sanitizeName(candidate.name);\n         const resolution = resolveCandidate(candidate, index);\n         if (resolution.action === 'skip') continue;\n         if (resolution.action === 'fuzzy') continue; // Skip fuzzy in background — safer\n\n         const key = resolution.key;\n         const content = resolution.action === 'merge' ? resolution.content : candidate.content;\n         const now = new Date().toISOString();\n         const existing = index.memories[key] as MemoryEntry | undefined;\n\n         index.memories[key] = {\n            ...(existing ?? ({} as MemoryEntry)),\n            name: candidate.name,\n            category: candidate.category,\n            description: candidate.content.slice(0, 80),\n            content: content,\n            hotness: existing?.hotness ?? 0.5,\n            active_count: existing?.active_count ?? 0,\n            created: existing?.created ?? now,\n            updated: now,\n            source_sessions: [...new Set([...(existing?.source_sessions ?? []), sessionId])],\n            projects: [...new Set([...(existing?.projects ?? []), project].filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p)))],\n         };\n\n         const categoryDir = join(memoriesDir, candidate.category);\n         mkdirSync(categoryDir, { recursive: true });\n         const memEntry = index.memories[key];\n         if (memEntry) {\n            writeFileSync(join(categoryDir, candidate.name + '.md'), serializeMemory(memEntry, content));\n         }\n      }\n\n      const sessionEntry = index.sessions[sessionId];\n      if (sessionEntry) {\n         sessionEntry.l1_ready = true;\n         sessionEntry.extracted_at = new Date().toISOString();\n      }\n      writeIndex(indexPath, index);\n   } catch (err) {\n      const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n      try {\n         appendFileSync(errorLog, `[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${sessionId}: ${errorMessage}\\n`);\n      } catch {\n         // Logging error — ignore\n      }\n      try {\n         const index = readIndex(indexPath);\n         if (index.sessions?.[sessionId]) {\n            index.sessions[sessionId].extraction_failed = true;\n            index.sessions[sessionId].extraction_attempts = (index.sessions[sessionId].extraction_attempts || 0) + 1;\n            writeIndex(indexPath, index);\n         }\n      } catch {\n         // Index update error — ignore\n      }\n   } finally {\n      releaseLock(lockPath);\n   }\n}\n\nif (process.argv[1] && process.argv[1] === new URL(import.meta.url).pathname) {\n   main().catch(() => process.exit(1));\n}\n","numLines":405,"startLine":1,"totalLines":405}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"59035361-f1b6-417a-8f79-c0f8b2dad02a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

test_empty_input_returns_empty_string() -> None:\n    assert clean_text(\"\") == \"\"\n\n\ndef test_no_tags_returns_trimmed_input() -> None:\n    assert clean_text(\"  hello world  \") == \"hello world\"\n\n\ndef test_outer_tag_strips_nested_inner() -> None:\n    \"\"\"snapshot.ts non-greedy match strips outer-first; inner tag becomes irrelevant.\"\"\"\n    text = \"[stripped]real\"\n    # {"parentUuid":"b73d3ef7-300d-4b22-8a44-67953593903e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01YAKkaRmehtjrtibzgt8ZJM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BeJJ47YQnqS1zpUr9bWyvM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\observability\\tests\\test_extract_l0.py","content":"\"\"\"Test extract_l0 — 5 extractor functions + VN failure patterns + JSONL aggregator.\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom packages.observability import (\n    ChatMessage,\n    extract_commands,\n    extract_errors,\n    extract_failures,\n    extract_file_paths,\n    extract_l0_from_jsonl,\n    extract_l0_from_messages,\n    extract_next_step,\n)\n\n\n# --- File paths ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_file_paths_finds_python_paths() -> None:\n    text = \"edit packages/observability/extract_l0.py and tests/test_x.py\"\n    paths = extract_file_paths(text)\n    assert \"packages/observability/extract_l0.py\" in paths\n    assert \"tests/test_x.py\" in paths\n\n\ndef test_extract_file_paths_skips_http_urls() -> None:\n    text = \"see http://example.com/path.html for details\"\n    assert \"http://example.com/path.html\" not in extract_file_paths(text)\n\n\ndef test_extract_file_paths_dedupes() -> None:\n    text = \"src/a.py and src/a.py again\"\n    paths = extract_file_paths(text)\n    assert paths.count(\"src/a.py\") == 1\n\n\n# --- Commands ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_commands_finds_bash_block() -> None:\n    text = \"```bash\\npytest -v\\n```\"\n    cmds = extract_commands(text)\n    assert \"pytest -v\" in cmds\n\n\ndef test_extract_commands_finds_dollar_prefix() -> None:\n    text = \"$ ls -la /tmp\"\n    cmds = extract_commands(text)\n    assert \"ls -la /tmp\" in cmds\n\n\ndef test_extract_commands_caps_at_20() -> None:\n    text = \"\\n\".join(f\"$ cmd{i}\" for i in range(50))\n    assert len(extract_commands(text)) <= 20\n\n\n# --- Errors ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_errors_finds_error_prefix() -> None:\n    text = \"Error: something exploded badly here\"\n    errs = extract_errors(text)\n    assert any(\"something exploded badly here\" in e for e in errs)\n\n\ndef test_extract_errors_finds_fatal() -> None:\n    text = \"FATAL: unrecoverable database corruption detected\"\n    errs = extract_errors(text)\n    assert any(\"unrecoverable database corruption\" in e for e in errs)\n\n\ndef test_extract_errors_caps_at_10() -> None:\n    text = \"\\n\".join(f\"Error: msg number {i:02d} that is long enough\" for i in range(20))\n    assert len(extract_errors(text)) <= 10\n\n\n# --- Failures (incl. VN extension per D-002 REV-2 § A) ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_english() -> None:\n    text = \"I tried using regex but it didn't work as expected.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"didn't work\" in f.lower() or \"tried using regex\" in f.lower() for f in fails)\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_russian() -> None:\n    text = \"не сработало с этим подходом, нужно другой.\"\n    assert len(extract_failures(text)) >= 1\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_vietnamese_khong_hoat_dong() -> None:\n    text = \"Cách này không hoạt động trên đa nền tảng.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"không hoạt động\" in f for f in fails)\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_vietnamese_khong_hieu_qua() -> None:\n    text = \"Phương pháp đó không hiệu quả lắm trên dữ liệu lớn.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"không hiệu quả\" in f for f in fails)\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_vietnamese_da_thu_nhung() -> None:\n    text = \"Tôi đã thử nhưng vẫn không ổn định.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"đã thử nhưng\" in f for f in fails)\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_vietnamese_khong_kha_thi() -> None:\n    text = \"Phương án này không khả thi vì chi phí quá cao.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"không khả thi\" in f for f in fails)\n\n\ndef test_extract_failures_vietnamese_that_bai() -> None:\n    text = \"Lần thử nghiệm đầu tiên thất bại do timeout.\"\n    fails = extract_failures(text)\n    assert any(\"thất bại\" in f for f in fails)\n\n\n# --- Next step ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_next_step_uses_explicit_marker() -> None:\n    msgs: list[ChatMessage] = [\n        {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"Done with phase 1. Next step: write the L1 prompt builder.\"}\n    ]\n    step = extract_next_step(msgs)\n    assert step is not None and \"L1 prompt builder\" in step\n\n\ndef test_extract_next_step_falls_back_to_first_sentence() -> None:\n    msgs: list[ChatMessage] = [\n        {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"We finished implementing the cache invalidation logic. More details follow.\"}\n    ]\n    step = extract_next_step(msgs)\n    assert step is not None and \"cache invalidation\" in step\n\n\ndef test_extract_next_step_returns_none_when_no_assistant() -> None:\n    msgs: list[ChatMessage] = [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"hi\"}]\n    assert extract_next_step(msgs) is None\n\n\n# --- Aggregator ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_l0_from_messages_summary_uses_first_user() -> None:\n    msgs: list[ChatMessage] = [\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Hello world request for help\"},\n        {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"Sure\"},\n    ]\n    l0 = extract_l0_from_messages(msgs, project=\"stockforge\")\n    assert l0[\"summary\"] == \"Hello world request for help\"\n    assert l0[\"project\"] == \"stockforge\"\n    assert l0[\"messageCount\"] == 2\n\n\ndef test_extract_l0_from_messages_empty() -> None:\n    l0 = extract_l0_from_messages([], project=\"stockforge\")\n    assert l0[\"messageCount\"] == 0\n    assert l0[\"summary\"] == \"\"\n\n\n# --- JSONL parser ---\n\n\ndef test_extract_l0_from_jsonl_fixture() -> None:\n    fixture = Path(__file__).parent / \"fixtures\" / \"sample-transcript.jsonl\"\n    lines = fixture.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n    l0 = extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project=\"stockforge\")\n    assert l0[\"messageCount\"] > 0\n    # Fixture has explicit Vietnamese failure phrases.\n    fails = l0.get(\"failures\") or []\n    assert any(\"không hoạt động\" in f for f in fails), f\"Expected VN không hoạt động in failures, got: {fails}\"\n    # Fixture mentions packages/observability/extract_l0.py multiple times.\n    files = l0.get(\"files\") or []\n    assert any(\"packages/observability/extract_l0.py\" in f for f in files)\n    # Fixture has bash commands.\n    cmds = l0.get(\"commands\") or []\n    assert len(cmds) > 0\n\n\ndef test_extract_l0_from_jsonl_skips_malformed_lines() -> None:\n    lines = [\n        '{\"type\":\"human\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"hello\"}}',\n        \"not json at all\",\n        '{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"hi\"}}',\n    ]\n    l0 = extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project=\"stockforge\")\n    assert l0[\"messageCount\"] == 2\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":307,"cache_read_input_tokens":149950,"output_tokens":8187,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":307,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":8187,"cache_read_input_tokens":149950,"cache_creation_input_tokens":307,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":307},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaYPLGGFR1EWxCEjw8SYH","type":"assistant","uuid":"5b28fd54-105c-4b4d-b112-a2d476b705f6","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:34:21.154Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"31b60350-d93a-4316-86ae-0cdc4b74436d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01YAKkaRmehtjrtibzgt8ZJM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EXcMMhHTj116wvnb6kGK2n","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\observability\\tests\\test_transcript_cache.py","content":"\"\"\"Test TranscriptCache — mtime+size dual-check + LRU eviction.\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom packages.observability import TranscriptCache\n\n\ndef _write(path: str, content: str) -> None:\n    Path(path).write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef _identity_parser(text: str) -> str | None:\n    return text if text else None\n\n\ndef _line_count_parser(text: str) -> int | None:\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    return len([line for line in text.split(\"\\n\") if line])\n\n\ndef test_extract_returns_none_for_missing_file() -> None:\n    cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n    assert cache.extract(\"/nonexistent/path.jsonl\") is None\n\n\ndef test_extract_returns_none_for_empty_path() -> None:\n    cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n    assert cache.extract(\"\") is None\n\n\ndef test_first_read_calls_parser() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"alpha\\nbeta\\ngamma\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 3\n        assert cache.size == 1\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_cache_hit_skips_parser() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\\nc\")\n        path = f.name\n    call_count = [0]\n\n    def counting_parser(text: str) -> int:\n        call_count[0] += 1\n        return len(text)\n\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(counting_parser)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        assert call_count[0] == 1, f\"parser called {call_count[0]} times; expected 1\"\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_file_grow_triggers_reread() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 2\n        time.sleep(0.05)  # ensure mtime delta on filesystems with sub-ms granularity\n        with open(path, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            f.write(\"\\nc\\nd\")\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 4\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_file_shrink_triggers_full_reread() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\\nc\\nd\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 4\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n        _write(path, \"x\")\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 1\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_invalidate_removes_entry() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        assert cache.size == 1\n        cache.invalidate(path)\n        assert cache.size == 0\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_clear_empties_cache() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(3):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n                paths.append(f.name)\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n        for p in paths:\n            cache.extract(p)\n        assert cache.size == 3\n        cache.clear()\n        assert cache.size == 0\n    finally:\n        for p in paths:\n            os.unlink(p)\n\n\ndef test_lru_eviction_at_max_entries() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(5):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n                paths.append(f.name)\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser, max_entries=3)\n        for p in paths:\n            cache.extract(p)\n        assert cache.size == 3\n        # First 2 paths should have been evicted.\n        stats = cache.stats()\n        assert paths[0] not in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[1] not in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[4] in stats[\"paths\"]\n    finally:\n        for p in paths:\n            os.unlink(p)\n\n\ndef test_lru_recency_promotes_on_access() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(3):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n TranscriptCache — mtime+size dual-check + LRU eviction.\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom packages.observability import TranscriptCache\n\n\ndef _write(path: str, content: str) -> None:\n    Path(path).write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef _identity_parser(text: str) -> str | None:\n    return text if text else None\n\n\ndef _line_count_parser(text: str) -> int | None:\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    return len([line for line in text.split(\"\\n\") if line])\n\n\ndef test_extract_returns_none_for_missing_file() -> None:\n    cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n    assert cache.extract(\"/nonexistent/path.jsonl\") is None\n\n\ndef test_extract_returns_none_for_empty_path() -> None:\n    cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n    assert cache.extract(\"\") is None\n\n\ndef test_first_read_calls_parser() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"alpha\\nbeta\\ngamma\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 3\n        assert cache.size == 1\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_cache_hit_skips_parser() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\\nc\")\n        path = f.name\n    call_count = [0]\n\n    def counting_parser(text: str) -> int:\n        call_count[0] += 1\n        return len(text)\n\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(counting_parser)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        assert call_count[0] == 1, f\"parser called {call_count[0]} times; expected 1\"\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_file_grow_triggers_reread() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 2\n        time.sleep(0.05)  # ensure mtime delta on filesystems with sub-ms granularity\n        with open(path, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            f.write(\"\\nc\\nd\")\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 4\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_file_shrink_triggers_full_reread() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\\nb\\nc\\nd\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_line_count_parser)\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 4\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n        _write(path, \"x\")\n        assert cache.extract(path) == 1\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_invalidate_removes_entry() -> None:\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"a\")\n        path = f.name\n    try:\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n        cache.extract(path)\n        assert cache.size == 1\n        cache.invalidate(path)\n        assert cache.size == 0\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(path)\n\n\ndef test_clear_empties_cache() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(3):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n                paths.append(f.name)\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser)\n        for p in paths:\n            cache.extract(p)\n        assert cache.size == 3\n        cache.clear()\n        assert cache.size == 0\n    finally:\n        for p in paths:\n            os.unlink(p)\n\n\ndef test_lru_eviction_at_max_entries() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(5):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n                paths.append(f.name)\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser, max_entries=3)\n        for p in paths:\n            cache.extract(p)\n        assert cache.size == 3\n        # First 2 paths should have been evicted.\n        stats = cache.stats()\n        assert paths[0] not in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[1] not in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[4] in stats[\"paths\"]\n    finally:\n        for p in paths:\n            os.unlink(p)\n\n\ndef test_lru_recency_promotes_on_access() -> None:\n    paths = []\n    try:\n        for i in range(3):\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n                mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n            ) as f:\n                f.write(f\"file{i}\")\n                paths.append(f.name)\n        cache = TranscriptCache(_identity_parser, max_entries=3)\n        for p in paths:\n            cache.extract(p)\n        # Access path[0] to promote it to most-recent.\n        cache.extract(paths[0])\n        # Add a 4th path → path[1] should be evicted (was least-recent after promotion).\n        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n            mode=\"w\", suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, encoding=\"utf-8\"\n        ) as f:\n            f.write(\"new\")\n            paths.append(f.name)\n        cache.extract(paths[3])\n        stats = cache.stats()\n        assert paths[0] in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[1] not in stats[\"paths\"]\n        assert paths[3] in stats[\"paths\"]\n    finally:\n        for p in paths:\n            os.unlink(p)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"71f6d63f-04e9-4e96-b5f0-93ca797ff0cc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

   for cat in (\"profile\", \"preferences\", \"entities\", \"events\", \"cases\", \"patterns\"):\n        assert cat in p, f\"category {cat} missing from prompt\"\n\n\ndef test_build_extraction_prompt_emphasizes_failed_approaches() -> None:\n    \"\"\"extract-l1.ts:53 — explicit emphasis on FAILED APPROACHES carry-over.\"\"\"\n    p = build_extraction_prompt([{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"x\"}])\n    assert \"FAILED APPROACHES\" in p\n\n\ndef test_build_extraction_prompt_has_json_array_marker() -> None:\n    p = build_extraction_prompt([{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"x\"}])\n    assert \"JSON array\" in p\n\n\ndef test_build_extraction_prompt_concatenates_messages() -> None:\n    msgs: list[ChatMessage] = [\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"first\"},\n        {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"second\"},\n    ]\n    p = build_extraction_prompt(msgs)\n    assert \"user: first\" in p\n    assert \"assistant: second\" in p\n\n\n# --- Parser ---\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_clean_json() -> None:\n    raw = '[{\"category\":\"events\",\"name\":\"test-event\",\"content\":\"ran tests at 10am\"}]'\n    out = parse_llm_response(raw)\n    assert len(out) == 1\n    assert out[0][\"category\"] == \"events\"\n    assert out[0][\"name\"] == \"test-event\"\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_with_prose_preamble() -> None:\n    raw = \"Here are the memories I extracted:\\n[{\\\"category\\\":\\\"profile\\\",\\\"name\\\":\\\"role\\\",\\\"content\\\":\\\"engineer\\\"}]\\nDone.\"\n    out = parse_llm_response(raw)\n    assert len(out) == 1\n    assert out[0][\"category\"] == \"profile\"\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_skips_invalid_category() -> None:\n    raw = '[{\"category\":\"BADCATEGORY\",\"name\":\"x\",\"content\":\"y\"},{\"category\":\"patterns\",\"name\":\"good\",\"content\":\"y\"}]'\n    out = parse_llm_response(raw)\n    assert len(out) == 1\n    assert out[0][\"category\"] == \"patterns\"\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_skips_missing_fields() -> None:\n    raw = out[0][\"name\"] == \"good\"\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_empty_input() -> None:\n    assert parse_llm_response(\"\") == []\n    assert parse_llm_response(\"   \") == []\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_no_json_array() -> None:\n    assert parse_llm_response(\"Just prose, no JSON here.\") == []\n\n\ndef test_parse_llm_response_malformed_json() -> None:\n    assert {"parentUuid":"b827e333-ab06-4d26-977d-5f4e1f12d3df","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ASxgiNswMNSL2VAaLyvcL3","type":"tool_result","content":"Updated task #5 status"}]},"uuid":"18893104-24c6-4ddb-8fe8-73c3499bd756","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:35:31.183Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"taskId":"5","updatedFields":["status"],"statusChange":{"from":"in_progress","to":"completed"},"verificationNudgeNeeded":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b827e333-ab06-4d26-977d-5f4e1f12d3df","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"18893104-24c6-4ddb-8fe8-73c3499bd756","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_0187BTrawGBSjqnyXKoBpdfZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01N8QW7FciWrW3PZMXw33mki","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\session-memory-l0-l1\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: session-memory-l0-l1\ndescription: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\nallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash]\n---\n\n# Session Memory L0/L1 Extraction\n\n> **Status**: Phase 0 library port per D-007 + D-002 REV-2 § A.\n> **Source repo**: `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/claude-sessions/src/memory/` (TS originals).\n> **Module**: `packages/observability/`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTransform a raw Claude Code session JSONL transcript into structured signals:\n\n- **L0 (deterministic)**: file paths touched / shell commands run / error messages / failure phrases / inferred next step. Pure regex. Runs in milliseconds; safe to fire on every SessionEnd.\n- **L1 (LLM-mediated)**: structured memory candidates across 6 categories (profile / preferences / entities / events / cases / patterns). Caller dispatches the LLM with the provided prompt; parser validates the response.\n- **TranscriptCache**: speeds repeat reads of the same transcript (mtime + size dual-check; LRU eviction; full re-parse on grow/shrink/rewrite).\n- **cleanText**: strips 9 Claude Code harness wrapper tags before extraction so memories aren't polluted with system-reminder / command-name / task-notification boilerplate.\n\n## When to Use\n\n1. **SessionEnd ingestion** — port a just-completed session's JSONL into the memory layer (Phase 1+ wire-in).\n2. **Calibration data prep** — need to know which files/commands/failures the session touched, no LLM call needed.\n3. **Offline L1 extraction** — assemble the LLM prompt, dispatch via your own client, feed response back through the validator.\n4. **Repeat-read scenario** — telemetry hooks reading the same transcript multiple times benefit from `TranscriptCache`.\n\n## When NOT to Use\n\n1. **Real-time** during the active session — the JSONL is mid-write; use post-SessionEnd.\n2. **Token usage / compaction telemetry** — out-of-scope. See `Claude-Code-Agent-Monitor/server/lib/transcript-cache.js` for that.\n3. **Sentiment / market analysis on session content** — wrong layer. This module does Claude Code session telemetry only, not stock-domain analysis.\n\n## API Quick Reference\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import (\n    # L0 — pure regex\n    extract_l0_from_jsonl,        # parse JSONL lines → L0Data\n    extract_l0_from_messages,     # if you already have messages\n    extract_file_paths,\n    extract_commands,\n    extract_errors,\n    extract_failures,             # incl. Vietnamese phrases per D-002 REV-2 § A\n    extract_next_step,\n    # L1 — prompt + parser + windowing\n    build_extraction_prompt,      # str → feed to LLM caller\n    parse_llm_response,           # validate response → list[MemoryCandidate]\n    extract_messages_from_jsonl,  # 15+35 head/tail windowing\n    HEAD_COUNT, TAIL_COUNT,\n    # Caching + cleaning\n    TranscriptCache,\n    clean_text,\n)\n```\n\n## Examples\n\n### Example 1 — L0 quick metadata\n\n```python\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom packages.observability import extract_l0_from_jsonl\n\nlines = Path(\"session.jsonl\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\nl0 = extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project=\"stockforge\")\n\nprint(l0[\"files\"])      # files touched\nprint(l0[\"commands\"])   # bash commands run\nprint(l0.get(\"failures\", []))  # incl. \"không hoạt động\" / \"thất bại\" etc.\nprint(l0.get(\"next_step\"))     # last assistant's next step marker\n```\n\n### Example 2 — L1 prompt build for offline LLM dispatch\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import (\n    build_extraction_prompt,\n    extract_messages_from_jsonl,\n    parse_llm_response,\n)\n\n# 15+35 head/tail windowing automatically applied for sessions > 50 messages.\nmessages = extract_messages_from_jsonl(\"session.jsonl\")\nprompt = build_extraction_prompt(messages)\n\n# Dispatch via your own client (Anthropic SDK, claude CLI, batch API, etc.).\nresponse_text = my_llm_client.complete(prompt)\n\n# Validate + filter.\ncandidates = parse_llm_response(response_text)\nfor c in candidates:\n    print(c[\"category\"], \"/\", c[\"name\"], \"—\", c[\"content\"])\n```\n\n### Example 3 — TranscriptCache for repeat reads\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import TranscriptCache, extract_l0_from_jsonl\n\ndef parse_l0(content: str):\n    return extract_l0_from_jsonl(content.splitlines(), project=\"stockforge\")\n\ncache = TranscriptCache(parse_l0, max_entries=100)\n\n# First call parses; subsequent calls hit cache unless mtime+size changed.\nl0 = cache.extract(\"session.jsonl\")\nl0_again = cache.extract(\"session.jsonl\")  # cache hit; parser not invoked\n\nprint(cache.size)     # 1\nprint(cache.stats())  # {\"entries\": 1, \"paths\": [...]}\n```\n\n## Inputs\n\n- **Transcript path**: absolute path to a Claude Code JSONL transcript.\n- **Project name**: identifier (string). Used as `L0Data.project` field. Stockforge canonical: `\"stockforge\"`.\n\n## Outputs\n\n| Function | Returns |\n|---|---|\n| `extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project)` | `L0Data` — TypedDict with files / commands / errors / failures / next_step |\n| `extract_messages_from_jsonl(path)` | `list[ChatMessage]` — windowed (HEAD+TAIL=50) |\n| `build_extraction_prompt(messages)` | `str` — prompt ready for LLM dispatch |\n| `parse_llm_response(text)` | `list[MemoryCandidate]` — validated; invalid candidates dropped |\n| `clean_text(text)` | `str` — stripped of 9 harness wrapper tags |\n| `TranscriptCache(parser).extract(path)` | parser's return type or `None` |\n\n## Source Schema Pointer\n\n- D-007 — `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/007-track-8b-memory-l0-l1-extraction.md`\n- D-002 REV-2 § A — original Track 8b spec\n- TS source — `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/claude-sessions/src/memory/{extract-l0,extract-l1,snapshot}.ts`\n- TranscriptCache origin — `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/Claude-Code-Agent-Monitor/server/lib/transcript-cache.js`\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n1. **Don't auto-invoke L1 from inside a hook**. L1 dispatch is Phase 1+ via Anthropic SDK or claude CLI. Hooks call L0 only — fast, deterministic.\n2. **Don't skip cleanText**. Without it, every L0 file-path extractor will pull paths out of `<system-reminder>` blocks (false positives) and L1 prompts will be polluted with harness boilerplate.\n3. **Don't parameterize FAILURE_PATTERNS at runtime**. The 11 patterns (RU + EN + VN) are stockforge-binding per D-002 REV-2 § A § B-8. Modify by editing `extract_l0.py` and running the test suite.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":418,"cache_read_input_tokens":167331,"output_tokens":3265,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":418,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3265,"cache_read_input_tokens":167331,"cache_creation_input_tokens":418,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":418},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaYPTvmD64xFDMWHnJMQk","type":"assistant","uuid":"fdc4d8bf-b9cd-4245-9064-1bd95dc92538","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:36:10.409Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"fdc4d8bf-b9cd-4245-9064-1bd95dc92538","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N8QW7FciWrW3PZMXw33mki","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\session-memory-l0-l1\\SKILL.md"}]},"uuid":"8bfd44fc-595c-447d-80dc-5c91270a32ec","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:36:10.828Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\skills\\session-memory-l0-l1\\SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: session-memory-l0-l1\ndescription: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\nallowed-tools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash]\n---\n\n# Session Memory L0/L1 Extraction\n\n> **Status**: Phase 0 library port per D-007 + D-002 REV-2 § A.\n> **Source repo**: `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/claude-sessions/src/memory/` (TS originals).\n> **Module**: `packages/observability/`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTransform a raw Claude Code session JSONL transcript into structured signals:\n\n- **L0 (deterministic)**: file paths touched / shell commands run / error messages / failure phrases / inferred next step. Pure regex. Runs in milliseconds; safe to fire on every SessionEnd.\n- **L1 (LLM-mediated)**: structured memory candidates across 6 categories (profile / preferences / entities / events / cases / patterns). Caller dispatches the LLM with the provided prompt; parser validates the response.\n- **TranscriptCache**: speeds repeat reads of the same transcript (mtime + size dual-check; LRU eviction; full re-parse on grow/shrink/rewrite).\n- **cleanText**: strips 9 Claude Code harness wrapper tags before extraction so memories aren't polluted with system-reminder / command-name / task-notification boilerplate.\n\n## When to Use\n\n1. **SessionEnd ingestion** — port a just-completed session's JSONL into the memory layer (Phase 1+ wire-in).\n2. **Calibration data prep** — need to know which files/commands/failures the session touched, no LLM call needed.\n3. **Offline L1 extraction** — assemble the LLM prompt, dispatch via your own client, feed response back through the validator.\n4. **Repeat-read scenario** — telemetry hooks reading the same transcript multiple times benefit from `TranscriptCache`.\n\n## When NOT to Use\n\n1. **Real-time** during the active session — the JSONL is mid-write; use post-SessionEnd.\n2. **Token usage / compaction telemetry** — out-of-scope. See `Claude-Code-Agent-Monitor/server/lib/transcript-cache.js` for that.\n3. **Sentiment / market analysis on session content** — wrong layer. This module does Claude Code session telemetry only, not stock-domain analysis.\n\n## API Quick Reference\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import (\n    # L0 — pure regex\n    extract_l0_from_jsonl,        # parse JSONL lines → L0Data\n    extract_l0_from_messages,     # if you already have messages\n    extract_file_paths,\n    extract_commands,\n    extract_errors,\n    extract_failures,             # incl. Vietnamese phrases per D-002 REV-2 § A\n    extract_next_step,\n    # L1 — prompt + parser + windowing\n    build_extraction_prompt,      # str → feed to LLM caller\n    parse_llm_response,           # validate response → list[MemoryCandidate]\n    extract_messages_from_jsonl,  # 15+35 head/tail windowing\n    HEAD_COUNT, TAIL_COUNT,\n    # Caching + cleaning\n    TranscriptCache,\n    clean_text,\n)\n```\n\n## Examples\n\n### Example 1 — L0 quick metadata\n\n```python\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom packages.observability import extract_l0_from_jsonl\n\nlines = Path(\"session.jsonl\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\nl0 = extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project=\"stockforge\")\n\nprint(l0[\"files\"])      # files touched\nprint(l0[\"commands\"])   # bash commands run\nprint(l0.get(\"failures\", []))  # incl. \"không hoạt động\" / \"thất bại\" etc.\nprint(l0.get(\"next_step\"))     # last assistant's next step marker\n```\n\n### Example 2 — L1 prompt build for offline LLM dispatch\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import (\n    build_extraction_prompt,\n    extract_messages_from_jsonl,\n    parse_llm_response,\n)\n\n# 15+35 head/tail windowing automatically applied for sessions > 50 messages.\nmessages = extract_messages_from_jsonl(\"session.jsonl\")\nprompt = build_extraction_prompt(messages)\n\n# Dispatch via your own client (Anthropic SDK, claude CLI, batch API, etc.).\nresponse_text = my_llm_client.complete(prompt)\n\n# Validate + filter.\ncandidates = parse_llm_response(response_text)\nfor c in candidates:\n    print(c[\"category\"], \"/\", c[\"name\"], \"—\", c[\"content\"])\n```\n\n### Example 3 — TranscriptCache for repeat reads\n\n```python\nfrom packages.observability import TranscriptCache, extract_l0_from_jsonl\n\ndef parse_l0(content: str):\n    return extract_l0_from_jsonl(content.splitlines(), project=\"stockforge\")\n\ncache = TranscriptCache(parse_l0, max_entries=100)\n\n# First call parses; subsequent calls hit cache unless mtime+size changed.\nl0 = cache.extract(\"session.jsonl\")\nl0_again = cache.extract(\"session.jsonl\")  # cache hit; parser not invoked\n\nprint(cache.size)     # 1\nprint(cache.stats())  # {\"entries\": 1, \"paths\": [...]}\n```\n\n## Inputs\n\n- **Transcript path**: absolute path to a Claude Code JSONL transcript.\n- **Project name**: identifier (string). Used as `L0Data.project` field. Stockforge canonical: `\"stockforge\"`.\n\n## Outputs\n\n| Function | Returns |\n|---|---|\n| `extract_l0_from_jsonl(lines, project)` | `L0Data` — TypedDict with files / commands / errors / failures / next_step |\n| `extract_messages_from_jsonl(path)` | `list[ChatMessage]` — windowed (HEAD+TAIL=50) |\n| `build_extraction_prompt(messages)` | `str` — prompt ready for LLM dispatch |\n| `parse_llm_response(text)` | `list[MemoryCandidate]` — validated; invalid candidates dropped |\n| `clean_text(text)` | `str` — stripped of 9 harness wrapper tags |\n| `TranscriptCache(parser).extract(path)` | parser's return type or `None` |\n\n## Source Schema Pointer\n\n- D-007 — `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/007-track-8b-memory-l0-l1-extraction.md`\n- D-002 REV-2 § A — original Track 8b spec\n- TS source — `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/claude-sessions/src/memory/{extract-l0,extract-l1,snapshot}.ts`\n- TranscriptCache origin — `C:/htdocs/orch-starter/Claude-Code-Agent-Monitor/server/lib/transcript-cache.js`\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n1. **Don't auto-invoke L1 from inside a hook**. L1 dispatch is Phase 1+ via Anthropic SDK or claude CLI. Hooks call L0 only — fast, deterministic.\n2. **Don't skip cleanText**. Without it, every L0 file-path extractor will pull paths out of `<system-reminder>` blocks (false positives) and L1 prompts will be polluted with harness boilerplate.\n3. **Don't parameterize FAILURE_PATTERNS at runtime**. The 11 patterns (RU + EN + VN) are stockforge-binding per D-002 REV-2 § A § B-8. Modify by editing `extract_l0.py` and running the test suite.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fdc4d8bf-b9cd-4245-9064-1bd95dc92538","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
