{"parentUuid":"f3d9639d-c5bf-4a02-8650-5123d584c251","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PJrNcKoKqMbmMYWmZhDio2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# sync-tracker-update.sh — Track 8a Confidence Score System update hook\n3\t# Per D-006 (IMPL-S17-1): bash+awk only; no python/jq/yq/sqlite3 (L-S11-1 portability).\n4\t#\n5\t# Usage:\n6\t#   sync-tracker-update.sh <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\n7\t#\n8\t# If <override_delta> is empty/omitted, the hook looks up `weight_<event_type>` from weights.yaml.\n9\t# This preserves the tunable-weights contract: edit weights.yaml once, all callers pick up new values.\n10\t# Override mode is for testing or non-standard partial-credit events.\n11\t#\n12\t# Categories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\n13\t# Event types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n14\t#              drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n15\t#\n16\t# Behavior:\n17\t#   1. Resolve delta from weights.yaml if not overridden.\n18\t#   2. Append event row to events.tsv (atomic append).\n19\t#   3. Recompute state.tsv from full events.tsv replay.\n20\t#   4. Decrement must_grill_remaining for the affected category if >0.\n21\t#   5. Call sync-tracker-render.sh to regenerate _index.md (best-effort; log if fails).\n22\t\n23\tset -euo pipefail\n24\t\n25\tROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\n26\tSYNC_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\n27\tEVENTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\n28\tSTATE=\"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\"\n29\tWEIGHTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\"\n30\tRENDER_HOOK=\"$ROOT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n31\tLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n32\t\n33\tCAT=\"${1:-}\"\n34\tEVT=\"${2:-}\"\n35\tOVERRIDE_DELTA=\"${3:-}\"\n36\tDEC_ID=\"${4:-}\"\n37\tSOURCE_EV=\"${5:-}\"\n38\tREASON=\"${6:-}\"\n39\t\n40\tif [[ -z \"$CAT\" || -z \"$EVT\" ]]; then"}]},"uuid":"35c36919-ce76-4e06-ad05-fa4b93e74501","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:36:25.420Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\sync-tracker-update.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# sync-tracker-update.sh — Track 8a Confidence Score System update hook\n# Per D-006 (IMPL-S17-1): bash+awk only; no python/jq/yq/sqlite3 (L-S11-1 portability).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   sync-tracker-update.sh <category> <event_type> [<override_delta>] [<decision_id> [<source_evidence> [<reason>]]]\n#\n# If <override_delta> is empty/omitted, the hook looks up `weight_<event_type>` from weights.yaml.\n# This preserves the tunable-weights contract: edit weights.yaml once, all callers pick up new values.\n# Override mode is for testing or non-standard partial-credit events.\n#\n# Categories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\n# Event types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n#              drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n#\n# Behavior:\n#   1. Resolve delta from weights.yaml if not overridden.\n#   2. Append event row to events.tsv (atomic append).\n#   3. Recompute state.tsv from full events.tsv replay.\n#   4. Decrement must_grill_remaining for the affected category if >0.\n#   5. Call sync-tracker-render.sh to regenerate _index.md (best-effort; log if fails).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nROOT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/../..\" && pwd)\"\nSYNC_DIR=\"$ROOT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\nEVENTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\nSTATE=\"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\"\nWEIGHTS=\"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\"\nRENDER_HOOK=\"$ROOT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nLOG=\"$ROOT_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nCAT=\"${1:-}\"\nEVT=\"${2:-}\"\nOVERRIDE_DELTA=\"${3:-}\"\nDEC_ID=\"${4:-}\"\nSOURCE_EV=\"${5:-}\"\nREASON=\"${6:-}\"\n\nif [[ -z \"$CAT\" || -z \"$EVT\" ]]; then","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":152}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f3d9639d-c5bf-4a02-8650-5123d584c251","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"35c36919-ce76-4e06-ad05-fa4b93e74501","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":48,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"bb7b822c-602a-4a1b-b32a-72de57adbbfb","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:36:25.453Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2a66ad07-18be-4656-8152-514c94dde09d","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NeE8hGV9XUtxezS4geQaTR","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\qa-answered-detector.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"bf00e5f9-a524-4487-95e4-2a1fb9b1b20d","timestamp":"2026-04-29T16:36:41.209Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\qa-answered-detector.sh","oldString":"PROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nLAST_SCAN_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-last-scan-ts\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"","newString":"PROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nLAST_SCAN_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-last-scan-ts\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nSYNC_HOOK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-update.sh\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# qa-answered-detector.sh — SessionStart hook flagging newly-moved answered bundles.\n# Detects bundles in human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/ with mtime > last-scan timestamp.\n# Emits notification line in .session-hooks.log so agent reads them at SessionStart.\n# Track 5 deliverable (deferred from Track 4 in S2).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nLAST_SCAN_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-last-scan-ts\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\n[ ! -d \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" ] && exit 0\n\nNOW_EPOCH=\"$(date -u +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# Read last scan timestamp; if missing, default to 1 day ago.\nLAST_SCAN=0\nif [ -f \"$LAST_SCAN_FILE\" ]; then\n  LAST_SCAN=\"$(tr -d '[:space:]' < \"$LAST_SCAN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$LAST_SCAN\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || LAST_SCAN=0\nfi\nif [ \"$LAST_SCAN\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  LAST_SCAN=$(( NOW_EPOCH - 86400 ))\nfi\n\nNEW_COUNT=0\nfor bundle in \"$ANSWERED_DIR\"/*.md; do\n  [ -f \"$bundle\" ] || continue\n  MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$bundle\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$bundle\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$MTIME\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || continue\n  if [ \"$MTIME\" -gt \"$LAST_SCAN\" ]; then\n    BUNDLE_NAME=\"$(basename \"$bundle\")\"\n    NEW_COUNT=$(( NEW_COUNT + 1 ))\n    echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] qa-answered-detector: NEW answered bundle: $BUNDLE_NAME (mtime=$(date -d \"@$MTIME\" -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo \"$MTIME\"))\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# Update last-scan timestamp.\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$NOW_EPOCH\" > \"$LAST_SCAN_FILE\"\n\n[ \"$NEW_COUNT\" -gt 0 ] && echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] qa-answered-detector: $NEW_COUNT new answered bundle(s) detected — agent should read them at session start\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":10,"oldLines":6,"newStart":10,"newLines":7,"lines":[" ANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\""," LAST_SCAN_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-last-scan-ts\""," HOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"","+SYNC_HOOK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-update.sh\""," "," [ ! -d \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" ] && exit 0"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2a66ad07-18be-4656-8152-514c94dde09d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"44913202-bf61-4169-942a-cd9e5616c8d9","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d3545fc6-ae79-4ed9-8bb3-c1b0cba55316","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014MrDUNN1ixRzTJwH1YMM9L","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# drift-signals-D1-D9.sh — composite drift detector running 9 grep-based signals.\n3\t# D1 = LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY per Q-A2 answer 2026-04-29: highest empirical firing\n4\t# rate at S2). D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n5\t# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n6\t# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n7\t# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\n8\tset -uo pipefail\n9\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n10\t\n11\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n12\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n13\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\n14\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n15\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n16\t\n17\tVIOLATIONS=0\n18\t\n19\temit() {\n20\t  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n21\t  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n22\t  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\t# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\n26\tcheck_loc() {\n27\t  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n28\t  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  local lines\n  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    else\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\n\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\ndone","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":165}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"44913202-bf61-4169-942a-cd9e5616c8d9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"8fb2d67a-9f33-4f7e-ac44-b6b4a8b11a2d","version":"2.1.122","gitBranch":"main"}

Subprocess invocation conflicts with: (a) Phase 0 deterministic-substrate-first principle; (b) Charter Principle 9 \"no LLM math\" (LLM only interprets); (c) brittleness on bash hook environments where claude binary may not be PATH-available.\n\n**Decision**: ship `build_extraction_prompt` + `parse_llm_response` + `extract_messages_from_jsonl` (windowing) as a library only. Caller (Phase 1+ Anthropic SDK client or claude-CLI bridge) composes dispatch on top. Phase 0 module is callable + smoke-testable today via fixture; no external CLI dependency.\n\n**Trade-off**: L1 extraction not auto-fired in Phase 0; Phase 1+ wire-in still needed (logged as carry-over to S19+).\n\n**Documented**: D-007 § Decision + § Why + Open Questions.\n\n### IMPL-S18-2: TranscriptCache — full re-parse on grow vs incremental merge (Phase 0 simplification)\n\n**Discovery**: source `transcript-cache.js` implements full incremental merge for token-extraction (parses bytes from `cached.bytesRead` to `stat.size`, merges per-model token counters into cached state). Porting that mechanic for the parameterized parser callable adds ~80 LOC complexity for a marginal gain (single extra full-file read on append events; for 10K-line transcripts this is microseconds).\n\n**Decision**: Phase 0 simplification — on grow/shrink/rewrite, do full re-parse. Cache-hit on identical mtime+size still avoids the read entirely (the dominant access pattern). Token-extraction-style incremental merge can be re-added later if profiling justifies; meanwhile the API surface stays simple.\n\n**Trade-off**: marginally higher I/O on rapid append-mode workloads; mitigated by mtime+size cache-hit being unaffected.\n\n**Documented**: this session log + transcript_cache.py module docstring.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons learned (1 candidate; L-S18-1)\n\n### L-S18-1: Cross-language regex porting requires explicit FAILURE_PATTERNS extension per identity locale\n\n**Rule**: when porting language-specific keyword regex from a source repo (TS / JS / Python / Go), do not assume the source's locale set covers the target project's identity. claude-sessions origin patterns were RU + EN (Son Nguyen / Andrey Bursukov authorship). Stockforge identity is VN-primary (per project memory + Charter \"Vietnamese stock market\"). Every imported keyword-regex tier must be audited for target-locale gaps and explicitly extended with target-locale phrases before shipping.\n\n**Reason**: missing target-locale patterns silently degrade extraction quality. Vietnamese failure phrases (\"không hoạt động\", \"thất bại\", etc.) are fingerprints stockforge cares about — not catching them in L0 means downstream calibration data is biased toward EN-only sessions, even when the actual session is Vietnamese.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- Pre-flight when porting: identify all keyword/phrase regex tiers (FAILURE_PATTERNS, NEXT_STEP_PATTERNS, ERROR_RE, etc.).\n- Audit each tier against project identity locale (VN for stockforge; en/vi as multi-locale for international projects).\n- Extend each tier with N target-locale phrases before shipping; cover via test cases (one per phrase minimum).\n- Document the locale extension in the port-decision file (D-NNN) so it shows up in drift/sandwich-verifier review.\n\n**Anti-example**: copying extract-l0.ts FAILURE_PATTERNS verbatim to Python without adding VN — drops 5 phrases of stockforge identity coverage; only EN/RU sessions register failures.\n\n**Correct example (this session)**: D-007 § Decision explicitly cites the 5 VN phrases as part of the decision; tests `test_extract_failures_vietnamese_*` cover each individually.\n\n**Promotion candidate**: amend `proposals/architecture-amendment.md` § \"When porting from source repos\" with a Rule N \"audit + extend identity-locale-specific regex tiers before ship\" sub-clause. Or extend `evidence-extraction/SKILL.md` with a \"Cross-locale port checklist\" section. Visible in available-skills system reminder post-write (✓ confirmed).\n\n### D-6 Option C coda — qa-answered-detector wire-in (DONE)\n\nEdited `scripts/hooks/qa-answered-detector.sh`:\n- Added `SYNC_HOOK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-update.sh\"` constant.\n- Inside the new-bundle detection loop (line 33-37 area), added 3-line block:\n  ```\n  if [ -x \"$SYNC_HOOK\" ]; then\n    \"$SYNC_HOOK\" DESIGN_THINKING q_and_a_resolution \"\" \"\" \"$BUNDLE_NAME\" \"qa bundle answered\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\" 2>&1 || true\n  fi\n  ```\n- Preserves SessionStart pipeline integrity via `\\|\\| true` (best-effort; sync-tracker failure won't block detector).\n- Final LOC 49 (≤180 hook budget).\n\n### D-7 Option C smoke test (DONE)\n\n```\n$ touch human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/2026-04-29-001-phase0-clusters.md\n$ echo \"0\" > agent-workspace/memory/.qa-last-scan-ts\n$ CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$(pwd)\" bash scripts/hooks/qa-answered-detector.sh\n$ tail -1 agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/events.tsv\n2026-04-29T16:37:05Z\\tDESIGN_THINKING\\tq_and_a_resolution\\t0.1\\t\\t2026-04-29-001-phase0-clusters.md\\tqa bundle answered\n$ awk '$1==\"DESIGN_THINKING\"' agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv\nDESIGN_THINKING\\t50.2\\t2\\t2026-04-29T16:37:05Z\\t0\n```\n\nPre-test: DESIGN_THINKING 50.1 / sample_count=1. Subprocess invocation conflicts with: (a) Phase 0 deterministic-substrate-first principle; (b) Charter Principle 9 \"no LLM math\" (LLM only interprets); (c) brittleness on bash hook environments where claude binary may not be PATH-available.\n\n**Decision**: ship `build_extraction_prompt` + `parse_llm_response` + `extract_messages_from_jsonl` (windowing) as a library only. Caller (Phase 1+ Anthropic SDK client or claude-CLI bridge) composes dispatch on top. Phase 0 module is callable + smoke-testable today via fixture; no external CLI dependency.\n\n**Trade-off**: L1 extraction not auto-fired in Phase 0; Phase 1+ wire-in still needed (logged as carry-over to S19+).\n\n**Documented**: D-007 § Decision + § Why + Open Questions.\n\n### IMPL-S18-2: TranscriptCache — full re-parse on grow vs incremental merge (Phase 0 simplification)\n\n**Discovery**: source `transcript-cache.js` implements full incremental merge for token-extraction (parses bytes from `cached.bytesRead` to `stat.size`, merges per-model token counters into cached state). Porting that mechanic for the parameterized parser callable adds ~80 LOC complexity for a marginal gain (single extra full-file read on append events; for 10K-line transcripts this is microseconds).\n\n**Decision**: Phase 0 simplification — on grow/shrink/rewrite, do full re-parse. Cache-hit on identical mtime+size still avoids the read entirely (the dominant access pattern). Token-extraction-style incremental merge can be re-added later if profiling justifies; meanwhile the API surface stays simple.\n\n**Trade-off**: marginally higher I/O on rapid append-mode workloads; mitigated by mtime+size cache-hit being unaffected.\n\n**Documented**: this session log + transcript_cache.py module docstring.\n\n---\n\n## Lessons learned (1 candidate; L-S18-1)\n\n### L-S18-1: Cross-language regex porting requires explicit FAILURE_PATTERNS extension per identity locale\n\n**Rule**: when porting language-specific keyword regex from a source repo (TS / JS / Python / Go), do not assume the source's locale set covers the target project's identity. claude-sessions origin patterns were RU + EN (Son Nguyen / Andrey Bursukov authorship). Stockforge identity is VN-primary (per project memory + Charter \"Vietnamese stock market\"). Every imported keyword-regex tier must be audited for target-locale gaps and explicitly extended with target-locale phrases before shipping.\n\n**Reason**: missing target-locale patterns silently degrade extraction quality. Vietnamese failure phrases (\"không hoạt động\", \"thất bại\", etc.) are fingerprints stockforge cares about — not catching them in L0 means downstream calibration data is biased toward EN-only sessions, even when the actual session is Vietnamese.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- Pre-flight when porting: identify all keyword/phrase regex tiers (FAILURE_PATTERNS, NEXT_STEP_PATTERNS, ERROR_RE, etc.).\n- Audit each tier against project identity locale (VN for stockforge; en/vi as multi-locale for international projects).\n- Extend each tier with N target-locale phrases before shipping; cover via test cases (one per phrase minimum).\n- Document the locale extension in the port-decision file (D-NNN) so it shows up in drift/sandwich-verifier review.\n\n**Anti-example**: copying extract-l0.ts FAILURE_PATTERNS verbatim to Python without adding VN — drops 5 phrases of stockforge identity coverage; only EN/RU sessions register failures.\n\n**Correct example (this session)**: D-007 § Decision explicitly cites the 5 VN phrases as part of the decision; tests `test_extract_failures_vietnamese_*` cover each individually.\n\n**Promotion candidate**: amend `proposals/architecture-amendment.md` § \"When porting from source repos\" with a Rule N \"audit + extend identity-locale-specific regex tiers before ship\" sub-clause.
